chunk-cut-paint.txt: hyper-oracle's paint-tier input for a WORD across an intra-line chunk cut (C17, 2026-10-09).
Lines 4 and 5 are each longer than 128 KiB with no newline: HyperLayout cuts them inside the line, before the
first ASCII byte at each 64 KiB target, and every target falls on the `e` of a `return`. IMMUTABLE: edit it and the gate silently weakens.
